+### SEMANTIC SURPRISE 1 — Wrong-kind catalog suppresses the per-ref resolution finding
+CLASS: SEMANTIC SURPRISE
+WHAT:  A declaration with `ListDecl.memberOf: keysOf("$.contextBlocks")` loads cleanly, but when the catalog is a list the engine emits only the catalog container finding and silently omits the declared per-ref membership finding.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `MembershipRule`/`ListDecl.memberOf` and `engine.ts` selector reliability; following contract C1's explicit statement that catalog failure does not suppress C7.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"wrong-kind-catalog-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: The wrong-kind catalog should yield its one container finding and the issued ref should independently yield `use.refs[0]`, `context block ref "alpha" does not resolve`, code `unresolved_context_block_ref` — two findings — because C1 expressly keeps C7 live; the loaded declaration yielded only one.
+
+### USABILITY 1 — `memberOf keysOf` cannot state entry-belted resolution
+CLASS: USABILITY
+WHAT:  I first expressed C7 with the only apparent ref-to-catalog form, list-level `memberOf` targeting `keysOf("$.contextBlocks")`; it loaded, but a ref to a key whose entry fails `{ body }` was treated as resolved.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `Selector`, `MembershipRule`, and `ListDecl.memberOf`; following contract C7's requirement that key existence alone is not resolution and that the entry must satisfy C3 by reference.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"malformed-entry-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: I expected two findings: the missing-body finding at `contextBlocks.alpha` and the declared unresolved-ref finding at `use.refs[0]`; `defineSurface` emitted no rejection explaining that `keysOf` observes existence only, and the run emitted just the entry finding.
+
+### CONFIRMED 4 — A defaulted optional catalog makes absent-catalog refs decidable
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  Declaring `default: {}` on the optional fixed-map field made an absent catalog behave as an empty catalog for membership without making catalog absence itself illegal.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `NodeBase.default` and `MembershipRule.whenOperandAbsent`; following contract C1/C7 and the documented one-time default materialization.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"absent-catalog-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"alpha\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"alpha\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: The absent catalog should not itself be an error, while each issued ref should still be decidable against an empty key set and fail at its own site; that happened on both channels.
+
+### CONFIRMED 5 — Member-shape failure excludes the member from resolution
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  A grammar-invalid ref member produced only its grammar finding and did not cascade into the list's membership lane.
+WHERE: `engine.ts` list evaluation and `vocabulary.ts` `ListDecl.member` plus `memberOf`; following contract C2/C7's exclusion of shape-failing members from resolution.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"malformed-ref-member","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: Exactly one grammar finding at index 0, with no unresolved-ref cascade, on both channels; that is exactly what happened.

function evalString(
  run: Run,
  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "string" }>,
  frame: Frame,
): NodeResult {
  const value = frame.value;
  const slots: Slots = { ...baseSlots(frame), grammar: decl.grammar?.re };
  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    // A node that declares NO type lane leaves the non-string case to its
    // membership lane, which emits ONE finding for both faults (the
    // measured form of `start` and of a transition target).
    const typeLane = decl.typeMessage ?? (decl.memberOf === undefined ? decl.grammar?.message : undefined);
    if (typeLane !== undefined) {
      run.emit(frame.path, render(typeLane, slots), decl.presence?.code);
      return { ok: false };
    }
  } else {
    if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && value.length === 0) {
      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots), decl.presence?.code);
      return { ok: false };
    }
    if (decl.grammar !== undefined && !new RegExp(decl.grammar.re, "u").test(value)) {
      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.grammar.message, slots));
      return { ok: false };
    }
  }
  if (decl.memberOf !== undefined) {
    const before = run.findings.length;
    checkMembership(run, frame, decl.memberOf, value, slots);
    if (run.findings.length > before) return { ok: false, value };
  }
  return typeof value === "string" ? { ok: true, value } : { ok: false };
}

function evalInteger(
  run: Run,
  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "integer" }>,
  frame: Frame,
): NodeResult {
  const value = frame.value;
  if (decl.sourceForm === "plainDecimalInteger" && run.channel.kind === "file") {
    const node: unknown = run.channel.doc.getIn(frame.segments, true);
    const message = sourceLadderFinding(node, run.channel.source, frame.path);
    if (message !== undefined) {
      run.emit(frame.path, message);
      return { ok: false };
    }
    // The ladder proves the SOURCE form; a declared resolved belt is a
    // separate obligation and still applies (its own bound may be
    // stricter than the ladder's built-in one).
  }
  const belt = decl.resolvedForm;
  if (belt?.safeInteger === true) {
    if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < belt.min) {
      run.emit(frame.path, render(belt.message, baseSlots(frame)), decl.presence?.code);
      return { ok: false };
    }
  }
  return { ok: true, value };
}

function evalEnum(
  run: Run,
  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "enum" }>,
  frame: Frame,
): NodeResult {

+        unknownMessage: "unknown use key {keyJson}; allowed: {keys}",
+        fields: { refs },
+      },
+    },
+  },
+  valueClasses: { blockId },
+  crossRules: [],
+  normalizers: [],
+};
+
+const surface = defineSurface(declaration);
+
+type Fixture = { name: string; direct: unknown; yaml: string };
+const fixtures: Fixture[] = [
+  {
+    name: "legal-resolved",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: { body: "hello" } }, use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha:\n    body: hello\nuse:\n  refs:\n    - alpha\n",
+  },
+  { name: "legal-absent-catalog-no-refs", direct: {}, yaml: "{}\n" },
+  { name: "legal-empty-catalog", direct: { contextBlocks: {} }, yaml: "contextBlocks: {}\n" },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-unresolved-ref",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: {}, use: { refs: ["missing"] } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks: {}\nuse:\n  refs: [missing]\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-malformed-id",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { Bad: { body: "x" } } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  Bad:\n    body: x\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-missing-body",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: {} } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha: {}\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-empty-body",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: { body: "" } } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha:\n    body: ''\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-nonstring-body",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: { body: 7 } } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha:\n    body: 7\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-catalog-not-map",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: [] },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks: []\n",
+  },
+];
+
+function direct(value: unknown) {
+  return runSurface(surface, value, { channel: { kind: "direct" } });
+}
+
+function file(yaml: string) {
+  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+  const value: unknown = doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true });
+  return runSurface(surface, value, { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml } });
+}
+
+if (process.argv[2] === "smoke") {
+  const item = fixtures[0]!;
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ loaded: Object.isFrozen(surface), name: item.name, direct: direct(item.direct).findings, file: file(item.yaml).findings }));
+} else if (process.argv[2] === "probes") {
+  const probes: Fixture[] = [
+    {
+      name: "wrong-kind-catalog-with-ref",
+      direct: { contextBlocks: [], use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+      yaml: "contextBlocks: []\nuse:\n  refs: [alpha]\n",
+    },
+    {
+      name: "malformed-entry-with-ref",
+      direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: {} }, use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+      yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha: {}\nuse:\n  refs: [alpha]\n",
+    },
+    {
+      name: "absent-catalog-with-ref",
+      direct: { use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+      yaml: "use:\n  refs: [alpha]\n",
+    },
+    {
+      name: "malformed-ref-member",
+      direct: { contextBlocks: {}, use: { refs: ["Bad"] } },
+      yaml: "contextBlocks: {}\nuse:\n  refs: [Bad]\n",
+    },
+  ];
+  for (const item of probes) {
+    const d = direct(item.direct);
+    const f = file(item.yaml);
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({
+      name: item.name,
+      direct: { count: d.findings.length, findings: d.findings },
+      file: { count: f.findings.length, findings: f.findings },
+      agree: JSON.stringify(d.findings) === JSON.stringify(f.findings),
+    }));
+  }
+} else {
+  for (const item of fixtures) {
+    const d = direct(item.direct);
+    const f = file(item.yaml);
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({
+      name: item.name,
+      direct: { count: d.findings.length, findings: d.findings },
+      file: { count: f.findings.length, findings: f.findings },
+      agree: JSON.stringify(d.findings) === JSON.stringify(f.findings),
+    }));
+  }
+}

+# Authoring exercise observations
+
+HEAD: `650eeddf274ee58db9ee81fd54326a32faaa40fb`
+
+### CONFIRMED 1 — A minimal catalog plus one resolving ref position loads and agrees across channels
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  `defineSurface` accepted the declaration and `runSurface` returned zero findings for the same valid catalog/ref value on both direct and parsed-YAML channels.
+WHERE: `defineSurface.ts` load-time gate and `engine.ts` `runSurface`; following `vocabulary.ts` `map.open`, `map.fixed`, `list`, `valueClass`, and `memberOf` doc comments.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts smoke`
+  `{"loaded":true,"name":"legal-resolved","direct":[],"file":[]}`
+EXPECTED: The declaration should load, be frozen, and a ref member equal to a `keysOf("$.contextBlocks")` key should produce no finding on either channel; that is exactly what happened.
+
+### CONFIRMED 2 — Optional and empty catalogs are legal when no refs are issued
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  Both an absent catalog and an explicitly empty catalog produced zero findings on both channels.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` field `default` plus `map.open`; following contract C1/C8(a-b) and the `NodeBase.default` documentation.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts`
+  `{"name":"legal-absent-catalog-no-refs","direct":{"count":0,"findings":[]},"file":{"count":0,"findings":[]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"legal-empty-catalog","direct":{"count":0,"findings":[]},"file":{"count":0,"findings":[]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: Both values should be legal and channel-symmetric; both produced exactly zero findings.
+
+### CONFIRMED 3 — The declared invalid-value lanes report the requested paths, messages, and counts
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  Unresolved refs, malformed ids, missing/empty/non-string bodies, and wrong-kind catalogs each produced exactly the one declared finding on both channels.
+WHERE: `engine.ts` evaluation of `ListDecl.memberOf`, `StringDecl.grammar`, `presence`, `nonempty`, type, and `MapOpenDecl.containerMessage`; following their `vocabulary.ts` doc comments.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts`
+  `{"name":"illegal-unresolved-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"missing\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"missing\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-malformed-id","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-missing-body","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-empty-body","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be nonempty"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be nonempty"}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-nonstring-body","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be a string; got 7"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be a string; got 7"}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-catalog-not-map","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: Each fixture should produce one finding at its declared grain, with the declaration's exact message (and C7 code for the unresolved ref), and the two channels should agree; all six did.
+
+### SEMANTIC SURPRISE 1 — Wrong-kind catalog suppresses the per-ref resolution finding
+CLASS: SEMANTIC SURPRISE
+WHAT:  A declaration with `ListDecl.memberOf: keysOf("$.contextBlocks")` loads cleanly, but when the catalog is a list the engine emits only the catalog container finding and silently omits the declared per-ref membership finding.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `MembershipRule`/`ListDecl.memberOf` and `engine.ts` selector reliability; following contract C1's explicit statement that catalog failure does not suppress C7.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"wrong-kind-catalog-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: The wrong-kind catalog should yield its one container finding and the issued ref should independently yield `use.refs[0]`, `context block ref "alpha" does not resolve`, code `unresolved_context_block_ref` — two findings — because C1 expressly keeps C7 live; the loaded declaration yielded only one.
+
+### USABILITY 1 — `memberOf keysOf` cannot state entry-belted resolution
+CLASS: USABILITY
+WHAT:  I first expressed C7 with the only apparent ref-to-catalog form, list-level `memberOf` targeting `keysOf("$.contextBlocks")`; it loaded, but a ref to a key whose entry fails `{ body }` was treated as resolved.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `Selector`, `MembershipRule`, and `ListDecl.memberOf`; following contract C7's requirement that key existence alone is not resolution and that the entry must satisfy C3 by reference.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"malformed-entry-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: I expected two findings: the missing-body finding at `contextBlocks.alpha` and the declared unresolved-ref finding at `use.refs[0]`; `defineSurface` emitted no rejection explaining that `keysOf` observes existence only, and the run emitted just the entry finding.
+
+### CONFIRMED 4 — A defaulted optional catalog makes absent-catalog refs decidable
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  Declaring `default: {}` on the optional fixed-map field made an absent catalog behave as an empty catalog for membership without making catalog absence itself illegal.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `NodeBase.default` and `MembershipRule.whenOperandAbsent`; following contract C1/C7 and the documented one-time default materialization.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"absent-catalog-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"alpha\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"alpha\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: The absent catalog should not itself be an error, while each issued ref should still be decidable against an empty key set and fail at its own site; that happened on both channels.
+
+### CONFIRMED 5 — Member-shape failure excludes the member from resolution
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  A grammar-invalid ref member produced only its grammar finding and did not cascade into the list's membership lane.
+WHERE: `engine.ts` list evaluation and `vocabulary.ts` `ListDecl.member` plus `memberOf`; following contract C2/C7's exclusion of shape-failing members from resolution.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"malformed-ref-member","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: Exactly one grammar finding at index 0, with no unresolved-ref cascade, on both channels; that is exactly what happened.
+
+### USABILITY 2 — A new surface needs unrelated substrate scaffolding with no minimal authoring form
+CLASS: USABILITY
+WHAT:  My first scaffolding choice was to import `templateFormat` and reuse its entire `substrate` object because `SurfaceDecl` requires every substrate branch even though this exercise declares only structural value rules.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `SurfaceDecl`/`SubstrateDecl` and `templateFormat.ts`'s surface object; no new-surface construction guidance exists outside the worked example.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts smoke`
+  `{"loaded":true,"name":"legal-resolved","direct":[],"file":[]}`
+EXPECTED: I expected either a documented reusable base/minimal constructor or guidance on whether a new surface should share versus restate substrate policy; there was no diagnostic because copying `templateFormat.substrate` passes the gate, so the worked example—not the construct documentation—supplied that decision.

+import { parseDocument } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/node_modules/yaml/dist/index.js";
+import { defineSurface } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/defineSurface.ts";
+import { runSurface } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts";
+import { templateFormat } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts";
+import type { NodeDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts";
+
+const rows = ["exercise"] as const;
+
+const blockId: NodeDecl = {
+  kind: "string",
+  tag: "vc-context-block-id",
+  rows,
+  typeMessage: "context block id must be a string; got {value}",
+  nonempty: { message: "context block id must be nonempty" },
+  grammar: {
+    re: "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$",
+    message: "invalid context block id {valueJson}; expected {grammar}",
+  },
+};
+
+const catalog: NodeDecl = {
+  kind: "map.open",
+  tag: "d-context-blocks",
+  rows,
+  default: {},
+  containerMessage: "contextBlocks must be a map; got {value}",
+  keyClass: {
+    kind: "valueClass",
+    tag: "d-context-block-key",
+    rows,
+    valueClass: "blockId",
+    label: "context block id",
+  },
+  keyLaneAt: "container",
+  entry: {
+    kind: "map.fixed",
+    tag: "d-context-block-entry",
+    rows,
+    containerMessage: "context block entry must be a map; got {value}",
+    missingMessage: "context block entry is missing required key {keyJson}",
+    unknownMessage: "unknown context block entry key {keyJson}; allowed: {keys}",
+    fields: {
+      body: {
+        kind: "string",
+        tag: "d-context-block-body",
+        rows,
+        presence: { required: true },
+        typeMessage: "context block body must be a string; got {value}",
+        nonempty: { message: "context block body must be nonempty" },
+      },
+    },
+  },
+};
+
+const refs: NodeDecl = {
+  kind: "list",
+  tag: "d-context-block-refs",
+  rows,
+  containerMessage: "refs must be a list; got {value}",
+  memberLaneAt: "index",
+  member: {
+    kind: "valueClass",
+    tag: "d-context-block-ref-member",
+    rows,
+    valueClass: "blockId",
+    label: "context block ref",
+  },
+  memberOf: {
+    relation: "memberOf",
+    target: { keysOf: "$.contextBlocks" },
+    code: "unresolved_context_block_ref",
+    message: "context block ref {valueJson} does not resolve",
+  },
+};
+
+const declaration: SurfaceDecl = {
+  substrate: templateFormat.substrate,
+  root: {
+    kind: "map.fixed",
+    tag: "d-exercise-root",
+    rows,
+    containerMessage: "document must be a map; got {value}",
+    unknownMessage: "unknown document key {keyJson}; allowed: {keys}",
+    fields: {
+      contextBlocks: catalog,
+      use: {
+        kind: "map.fixed",
+        tag: "d-use",
+        rows,
+        containerMessage: "use must be a map; got {value}",
+        unknownMessage: "unknown use key {keyJson}; allowed: {keys}",
+        fields: { refs },
+      },
+    },
+  },
+  valueClasses: { blockId },
+  crossRules: [],
+  normalizers: [],
+};
+
+const surface = defineSurface(declaration);
+
+type Fixture = { name: string; direct: unknown; yaml: string };
+const fixtures: Fixture[] = [
+  {
+    name: "legal-resolved",

+  `{"name":"illegal-unresolved-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"missing\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"missing\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-malformed-id","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-missing-body","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-empty-body","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be nonempty"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be nonempty"}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-nonstring-body","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be a string; got 7"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be a string; got 7"}]},"agree":true}`

+EXPECTED: Each fixture should produce one finding at its declared grain, with the declaration's exact message (and C7 code for the unresolved ref), and the two channels should agree; all six did.
+
+### SEMANTIC SURPRISE 1 — Wrong-kind catalog suppresses the per-ref resolution finding
+CLASS: SEMANTIC SURPRISE
+WHAT:  A declaration with `ListDecl.memberOf: keysOf("$.contextBlocks")` loads cleanly, but when the catalog is a list the engine emits only the catalog container finding and silently omits the declared per-ref membership finding.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `MembershipRule`/`ListDecl.memberOf` and `engine.ts` selector reliability; following contract C1's explicit statement that catalog failure does not suppress C7.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"wrong-kind-catalog-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: The wrong-kind catalog should yield its one container finding and the issued ref should independently yield `use.refs[0]`, `context block ref "alpha" does not resolve`, code `unresolved_context_block_ref` — two findings — because C1 expressly keeps C7 live; the loaded declaration yielded only one.
+
+### USABILITY 1 — `memberOf keysOf` cannot state entry-belted resolution
+CLASS: USABILITY
+WHAT:  I first expressed C7 with the only apparent ref-to-catalog form, list-level `memberOf` targeting `keysOf("$.contextBlocks")`; it loaded, but a ref to a key whose entry fails `{ body }` was treated as resolved.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `Selector`, `MembershipRule`, and `ListDecl.memberOf`; following contract C7's requirement that key existence alone is not resolution and that the entry must satisfy C3 by reference.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"malformed-entry-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: I expected two findings: the missing-body finding at `contextBlocks.alpha` and the declared unresolved-ref finding at `use.refs[0]`; `defineSurface` emitted no rejection explaining that `keysOf` observes existence only, and the run emitted just the entry finding.
+
+### CONFIRMED 4 — A defaulted optional catalog makes absent-catalog refs decidable
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  Declaring `default: {}` on the optional fixed-map field made an absent catalog behave as an empty catalog for membership without making catalog absence itself illegal.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `NodeBase.default` and `MembershipRule.whenOperandAbsent`; following contract C1/C7 and the documented one-time default materialization.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"absent-catalog-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"alpha\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"alpha\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: The absent catalog should not itself be an error, while each issued ref should still be decidable against an empty key set and fail at its own site; that happened on both channels.
+
+### CONFIRMED 5 — Member-shape failure excludes the member from resolution
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  A grammar-invalid ref member produced only its grammar finding and did not cascade into the list's membership lane.
+WHERE: `engine.ts` list evaluation and `vocabulary.ts` `ListDecl.member` plus `memberOf`; following contract C2/C7's exclusion of shape-failing members from resolution.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"malformed-ref-member","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: Exactly one grammar finding at index 0, with no unresolved-ref cascade, on both channels; that is exactly what happened.
+
+### USABILITY 2 — A new surface needs unrelated substrate scaffolding with no minimal authoring form
+CLASS: USABILITY
+WHAT:  My first scaffolding choice was to import `templateFormat` and reuse its entire `substrate` object because `SurfaceDecl` requires every substrate branch even though this exercise declares only structural value rules.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `SurfaceDecl`/`SubstrateDecl` and `templateFormat.ts`'s surface object; no new-surface construction guidance exists outside the worked example.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts smoke`
+  `{"loaded":true,"name":"legal-resolved","direct":[],"file":[]}`
+EXPECTED: I expected either a documented reusable base/minimal constructor or guidance on whether a new surface should share versus restate substrate policy; there was no diagnostic because copying `templateFormat.substrate` passes the gate, so the worked example—not the construct documentation—supplied that decision.

+import { parseDocument } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/node_modules/yaml/dist/index.js";
+import { defineSurface } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/defineSurface.ts";
+import { runSurface } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts";
+import { templateFormat } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts";
+import type { NodeDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts";
+
+const rows = ["exercise"] as const;
+
+const blockId: NodeDecl = {
+  kind: "string",
+  tag: "vc-context-block-id",
+  rows,
+  typeMessage: "context block id must be a string; got {value}",
+  nonempty: { message: "context block id must be nonempty" },
+  grammar: {
+    re: "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$",
+    message: "invalid context block id {valueJson}; expected {grammar}",
+  },
+};
+
+const catalog: NodeDecl = {
+  kind: "map.open",
+  tag: "d-context-blocks",
+  rows,
+  default: {},
+  containerMessage: "contextBlocks must be a map; got {value}",
+  keyClass: {
+    kind: "valueClass",
+    tag: "d-context-block-key",
+    rows,
+    valueClass: "blockId",
+    label: "context block id",
+  },
+  keyLaneAt: "container",
+  entry: {
+    kind: "map.fixed",
+    tag: "d-context-block-entry",
+    rows,
+    containerMessage: "context block entry must be a map; got {value}",
+    missingMessage: "context block entry is missing required key {keyJson}",
+    unknownMessage: "unknown context block entry key {keyJson}; allowed: {keys}",
+    fields: {
+      body: {
+        kind: "string",
+        tag: "d-context-block-body",
+        rows,
+        presence: { required: true },
+        typeMessage: "context block body must be a string; got {value}",
+        nonempty: { message: "context block body must be nonempty" },
+      },
+    },
+  },
+};
+
+const refs: NodeDecl = {
+  kind: "list",
+  tag: "d-context-block-refs",
+  rows,
+  containerMessage: "refs must be a list; got {value}",
+  memberLaneAt: "index",
+  member: {
+    kind: "valueClass",
+    tag: "d-context-block-ref-member",
+    rows,
+    valueClass: "blockId",
+    label: "context block ref",
+  },
+  memberOf: {
+    relation: "memberOf",
+    target: { keysOf: "$.contextBlocks" },
+    code: "unresolved_context_block_ref",
+    message: "context block ref {valueJson} does not resolve",
+  },
+};
+
+const declaration: SurfaceDecl = {
+  substrate: templateFormat.substrate,
+  root: {
+    kind: "map.fixed",
+    tag: "d-exercise-root",
+    rows,
+    containerMessage: "document must be a map; got {value}",
+    unknownMessage: "unknown document key {keyJson}; allowed: {keys}",
+    fields: {
+      contextBlocks: catalog,
+      use: {
+        kind: "map.fixed",
+        tag: "d-use",
+        rows,
+        containerMessage: "use must be a map; got {value}",
+        unknownMessage: "unknown use key {keyJson}; allowed: {keys}",
+        fields: { refs },
+      },
+    },
+  },
+  valueClasses: { blockId },
+  crossRules: [],
+  normalizers: [],
+};
+
+const surface = defineSurface(declaration);
+
+type Fixture = { name: string; direct: unknown; yaml: string };
+const fixtures: Fixture[] = [
+  {
+    name: "legal-resolved",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: { body: "hello" } }, use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha:\n    body: hello\nuse:\n  refs:\n    - alpha\n",
+  },
+  { name: "legal-absent-catalog-no-refs", direct: {}, yaml: "{}\n" },

+    direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: { body: 7 } } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha:\n    body: 7\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-catalog-not-map",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: [] },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks: []\n",
+  },
+];
+
+function direct(value: unknown) {
+  return runSurface(surface, value, { channel: { kind: "direct" } });
+}
+
+function file(yaml: string) {
+  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+  const value: unknown = doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true });
+  return runSurface(surface, value, { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml } });
+}
+
+if (process.argv[2] === "gate") {
+  const broken = structuredClone(declaration) as any;
+  broken.root.fields.use.fields.refs.memberOf.target = { keysOf: "$.contextBlockz" };
+  try {
+    defineSurface(broken);
+    console.log("unexpectedly loaded");
+  } catch (error) {
+    console.log(String(error));
+  }
+} else if (process.argv[2] === "smoke") {
+  const item = fixtures[0]!;

+} else if (process.argv[2] === "probes") {
+  const probes: Fixture[] = [
+    {
+      name: "wrong-kind-catalog-with-ref",
+      direct: { contextBlocks: [], use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+      yaml: "contextBlocks: []\nuse:\n  refs: [alpha]\n",
+    },
+    {
+      name: "malformed-entry-with-ref",
+      direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: {} }, use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+      yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha: {}\nuse:\n  refs: [alpha]\n",
+    },
+    {
+      name: "absent-catalog-with-ref",
+      direct: { use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+      yaml: "use:\n  refs: [alpha]\n",
+    },
+    {
+      name: "malformed-ref-member",
+      direct: { contextBlocks: {}, use: { refs: ["Bad"] } },
+      yaml: "contextBlocks: {}\nuse:\n  refs: [Bad]\n",
+    },
+  ];
+  for (const item of probes) {
+    const d = direct(item.direct);
+    const f = file(item.yaml);
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({
+      name: item.name,
+      direct: { count: d.findings.length, findings: d.findings },
+      file: { count: f.findings.length, findings: f.findings },
+      agree: JSON.stringify(d.findings) === JSON.stringify(f.findings),
+    }));
+  }
+} else {
+  for (const item of fixtures) {
+    const d = direct(item.direct);
+    const f = file(item.yaml);
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({
+      name: item.name,
+      direct: { count: d.findings.length, findings: d.findings },
+      file: { count: f.findings.length, findings: f.findings },
+      agree: JSON.stringify(d.findings) === JSON.stringify(f.findings),
+    }));
+  }
+}

+import { parseDocument } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/node_modules/yaml/dist/index.js";
+import { defineSurface } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/defineSurface.ts";
+import { runSurface } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts";
+import { templateFormat } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts";
+import type { NodeDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts";
+
+const rows = ["exercise"] as const;
+
+const blockId: NodeDecl = {
+  kind: "string",
+  tag: "vc-context-block-id",
+  rows,
+  typeMessage: "context block id must be a string; got {value}",
+  nonempty: { message: "context block id must be nonempty" },
+  grammar: {
+    re: "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$",
+    message: "invalid context block id {valueJson}; expected {grammar}",
+  },
+};
+
+const catalog: NodeDecl = {
+  kind: "map.open",
+  tag: "d-context-blocks",
+  rows,
+  default: {},
+  containerMessage: "contextBlocks must be a map; got {value}",
+  keyClass: {
+    kind: "valueClass",
+    tag: "d-context-block-key",
+    rows,
+    valueClass: "blockId",
+    label: "context block id",
+  },
+  keyLaneAt: "container",
+  entry: {
+    kind: "map.fixed",
+    tag: "d-context-block-entry",
+    rows,
+    containerMessage: "context block entry must be a map; got {value}",
+    missingMessage: "context block entry is missing required key {keyJson}",
+    unknownMessage: "unknown context block entry key {keyJson}; allowed: {keys}",
+    fields: {
+      body: {
+        kind: "string",
+        tag: "d-context-block-body",
+        rows,
+        presence: { required: true },
+        typeMessage: "context block body must be a string; got {value}",
+        nonempty: { message: "context block body must be nonempty" },
+      },
+    },
+  },
+};
+
+const refs: NodeDecl = {
+  kind: "list",
+  tag: "d-context-block-refs",
+  rows,
+  containerMessage: "refs must be a list; got {value}",
+  memberLaneAt: "index",
+  member: {
+    kind: "valueClass",
+    tag: "d-context-block-ref-member",
+    rows,
+    valueClass: "blockId",
+    label: "context block ref",
+  },
+  memberOf: {
+    relation: "memberOf",
+    target: { keysOf: "$.contextBlocks" },
+    code: "unresolved_context_block_ref",
+    message: "context block ref {valueJson} does not resolve",
+  },
+};
+
+const declaration: SurfaceDecl = {
+  substrate: templateFormat.substrate,
+  root: {
+    kind: "map.fixed",
+    tag: "d-exercise-root",
+    rows,
+    containerMessage: "document must be a map; got {value}",
+    unknownMessage: "unknown document key {keyJson}; allowed: {keys}",
+    fields: {
+      contextBlocks: catalog,
+      use: {
+        kind: "map.fixed",
+        tag: "d-use",
+        rows,
+        containerMessage: "use must be a map; got {value}",
+        unknownMessage: "unknown use key {keyJson}; allowed: {keys}",
+        fields: { refs },
+      },
+    },
+  },
+  valueClasses: { blockId },
+  crossRules: [],
+  normalizers: [],
+};
+
+const surface = defineSurface(declaration);
+
+type Fixture = { name: string; direct: unknown; yaml: string };
+const fixtures: Fixture[] = [
+  {
+    name: "legal-resolved",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: { body: "hello" } }, use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha:\n    body: hello\nuse:\n  refs:\n    - alpha\n",
+  },
+  { name: "legal-absent-catalog-no-refs", direct: {}, yaml: "{}\n" },
+  { name: "legal-empty-catalog", direct: { contextBlocks: {} }, yaml: "contextBlocks: {}\n" },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-unresolved-ref",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: {}, use: { refs: ["missing"] } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks: {}\nuse:\n  refs: [missing]\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-malformed-id",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { Bad: { body: "x" } } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  Bad:\n    body: x\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-missing-body",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: {} } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha: {}\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-empty-body",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: { body: "" } } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha:\n    body: ''\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-nonstring-body",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: { body: 7 } } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha:\n    body: 7\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-catalog-not-map",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: [] },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks: []\n",
+  },
+];
+
+function direct(value: unknown) {
+  return runSurface(surface, value, { channel: { kind: "direct" } });
+}
+
+function file(yaml: string) {
+  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+  const value: unknown = doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true });
+  return runSurface(surface, value, { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml } });
+}
+
+if (process.argv[2] === "gate") {
+  const broken = structuredClone(declaration) as any;
+  broken.root.fields.use.fields.refs.memberOf.target = { keysOf: "$.contextBlockz" };
+  try {
+    defineSurface(broken);
+    console.log("unexpectedly loaded");
+  } catch (error) {
+    console.log(String(error));
+  }
+} else if (process.argv[2] === "smoke") {
+  const item = fixtures[0]!;
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ loaded: Object.isFrozen(surface), name: item.name, direct: direct(item.direct).findings, file: file(item.yaml).findings }));
+} else if (process.argv[2] === "probes") {
+  const probes: Fixture[] = [
+    {
+      name: "wrong-kind-catalog-with-ref",
+      direct: { contextBlocks: [], use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+      yaml: "contextBlocks: []\nuse:\n  refs: [alpha]\n",
+    },
+    {
+      name: "malformed-entry-with-ref",
+      direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: {} }, use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+      yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha: {}\nuse:\n  refs: [alpha]\n",
+    },
+    {
+      name: "absent-catalog-with-ref",
+      direct: { use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+      yaml: "use:\n  refs: [alpha]\n",
+    },
+    {
+      name: "malformed-ref-member",
+      direct: { contextBlocks: {}, use: { refs: ["Bad"] } },
+      yaml: "contextBlocks: {}\nuse:\n  refs: [Bad]\n",
+    },
+  ];
+  for (const item of probes) {
+    const d = direct(item.direct);
+    const f = file(item.yaml);
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({
+      name: item.name,
+      direct: { count: d.findings.length, findings: d.findings },
+      file: { count: f.findings.length, findings: f.findings },
+      agree: JSON.stringify(d.findings) === JSON.stringify(f.findings),
+    }));
+  }
+} else {
+  for (const item of fixtures) {
+    const d = direct(item.direct);
+    const f = file(item.yaml);
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({
+      name: item.name,
+      direct: { count: d.findings.length, findings: d.findings },
+      file: { count: f.findings.length, findings: f.findings },
+      agree: JSON.stringify(d.findings) === JSON.stringify(f.findings),
+    }));
+  }
+}

+# Authoring exercise observations
+
+HEAD: `650eeddf274ee58db9ee81fd54326a32faaa40fb`
+
+### CONFIRMED 1 — A minimal catalog plus one resolving ref position loads and agrees across channels
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  `defineSurface` accepted the declaration and `runSurface` returned zero findings for the same valid catalog/ref value on both direct and parsed-YAML channels.
+WHERE: `defineSurface.ts` load-time gate and `engine.ts` `runSurface`; following `vocabulary.ts` `map.open`, `map.fixed`, `list`, `valueClass`, and `memberOf` doc comments.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts smoke`
+  `{"loaded":true,"name":"legal-resolved","direct":[],"file":[]}`
+EXPECTED: The declaration should load, be frozen, and a ref member equal to a `keysOf("$.contextBlocks")` key should produce no finding on either channel; that is exactly what happened.
+
+### CONFIRMED 2 — Optional and empty catalogs are legal when no refs are issued
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  Both an absent catalog and an explicitly empty catalog produced zero findings on both channels.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` field `default` plus `map.open`; following contract C1/C8(a-b) and the `NodeBase.default` documentation.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts`
+  `{"name":"legal-absent-catalog-no-refs","direct":{"count":0,"findings":[]},"file":{"count":0,"findings":[]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"legal-empty-catalog","direct":{"count":0,"findings":[]},"file":{"count":0,"findings":[]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: Both values should be legal and channel-symmetric; both produced exactly zero findings.
+
+### CONFIRMED 3 — The declared invalid-value lanes report the requested paths, messages, and counts
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  Unresolved refs, malformed ids, missing/empty/non-string bodies, and wrong-kind catalogs each produced exactly the one declared finding on both channels.
+WHERE: `engine.ts` evaluation of `ListDecl.memberOf`, `StringDecl.grammar`, `presence`, `nonempty`, type, and `MapOpenDecl.containerMessage`; following their `vocabulary.ts` doc comments.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts`
+  `{"name":"illegal-unresolved-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"missing\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"missing\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-malformed-id","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-missing-body","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-empty-body","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be nonempty"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be nonempty"}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-nonstring-body","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be a string; got 7"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be a string; got 7"}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-catalog-not-map","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: Each fixture should produce one finding at its declared grain, with the declaration's exact message (and C7 code for the unresolved ref), and the two channels should agree; all six did.
+
+### SEMANTIC SURPRISE 1 — Wrong-kind catalog suppresses the per-ref resolution finding
+CLASS: SEMANTIC SURPRISE
+WHAT:  A declaration with `ListDecl.memberOf: keysOf("$.contextBlocks")` loads cleanly, but when the catalog is a list the engine emits only the catalog container finding and silently omits the declared per-ref membership finding.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `MembershipRule`/`ListDecl.memberOf` and `engine.ts` selector reliability; following contract C1's explicit statement that catalog failure does not suppress C7.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"wrong-kind-catalog-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: The wrong-kind catalog should yield its one container finding and the issued ref should independently yield `use.refs[0]`, `context block ref "alpha" does not resolve`, code `unresolved_context_block_ref` — two findings — because C1 expressly keeps C7 live; the loaded declaration yielded only one.
+
+### USABILITY 1 — `memberOf keysOf` cannot state entry-belted resolution
+CLASS: USABILITY
+WHAT:  I first expressed C7 with the only apparent ref-to-catalog form, list-level `memberOf` targeting `keysOf("$.contextBlocks")`; it loaded, but a ref to a key whose entry fails `{ body }` was treated as resolved.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `Selector`, `MembershipRule`, and `ListDecl.memberOf`; following contract C7's requirement that key existence alone is not resolution and that the entry must satisfy C3 by reference.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"malformed-entry-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: I expected two findings: the missing-body finding at `contextBlocks.alpha` and the declared unresolved-ref finding at `use.refs[0]`; `defineSurface` emitted no rejection explaining that `keysOf` observes existence only, and the run emitted just the entry finding.
+
+### CONFIRMED 4 — A defaulted optional catalog makes absent-catalog refs decidable
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  Declaring `default: {}` on the optional fixed-map field made an absent catalog behave as an empty catalog for membership without making catalog absence itself illegal.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `NodeBase.default` and `MembershipRule.whenOperandAbsent`; following contract C1/C7 and the documented one-time default materialization.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"absent-catalog-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"alpha\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"alpha\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: The absent catalog should not itself be an error, while each issued ref should still be decidable against an empty key set and fail at its own site; that happened on both channels.
+
+### CONFIRMED 5 — Member-shape failure excludes the member from resolution
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  A grammar-invalid ref member produced only its grammar finding and did not cascade into the list's membership lane.
+WHERE: `engine.ts` list evaluation and `vocabulary.ts` `ListDecl.member` plus `memberOf`; following contract C2/C7's exclusion of shape-failing members from resolution.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"malformed-ref-member","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: Exactly one grammar finding at index 0, with no unresolved-ref cascade, on both channels; that is exactly what happened.
+
+### USABILITY 2 — A new surface needs unrelated substrate scaffolding with no minimal authoring form
+CLASS: USABILITY
+WHAT:  My first scaffolding choice was to import `templateFormat` and reuse its entire `substrate` object because `SurfaceDecl` requires every substrate branch even though this exercise declares only structural value rules.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `SurfaceDecl`/`SubstrateDecl` and `templateFormat.ts`'s surface object; no new-surface construction guidance exists outside the worked example.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts smoke`
+  `{"loaded":true,"name":"legal-resolved","direct":[],"file":[]}`
+EXPECTED: I expected either a documented reusable base/minimal constructor or guidance on whether a new surface should share versus restate substrate policy; there was no diagnostic because copying `templateFormat.substrate` passes the gate, so the worked example—not the construct documentation—supplied that decision.

apply patch

+    },

+# Authoring exercise observations
+
+HEAD: `650eeddf274ee58db9ee81fd54326a32faaa40fb`
+
+### CONFIRMED 1 — A minimal catalog plus one resolving ref position loads and agrees across channels
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  `defineSurface` accepted the declaration and `runSurface` returned zero findings for the same valid catalog/ref value on both direct and parsed-YAML channels.
+WHERE: `defineSurface.ts` load-time gate and `engine.ts` `runSurface`; following `vocabulary.ts` `map.open`, `map.fixed`, `list`, `valueClass`, and `memberOf` doc comments.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts smoke`
+  `{"loaded":true,"name":"legal-resolved","direct":[],"file":[]}`
+EXPECTED: The declaration should load, be frozen, and a ref member equal to a `keysOf("$.contextBlocks")` key should produce no finding on either channel; that is exactly what happened.
+
+### CONFIRMED 2 — Optional and empty catalogs are legal when no refs are issued
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  Both an absent catalog and an explicitly empty catalog produced zero findings on both channels.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` field `default` plus `map.open`; following contract C1/C8(a-b) and the `NodeBase.default` documentation.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts`
+  `{"name":"legal-absent-catalog-no-refs","direct":{"count":0,"findings":[]},"file":{"count":0,"findings":[]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"legal-empty-catalog","direct":{"count":0,"findings":[]},"file":{"count":0,"findings":[]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: Both values should be legal and channel-symmetric; both produced exactly zero findings.
+
+### CONFIRMED 3 — The declared invalid-value lanes report the requested paths, messages, and counts
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  Unresolved refs, malformed ids, missing/empty/non-string bodies, and wrong-kind catalogs each produced exactly the one declared finding on both channels.
+WHERE: `engine.ts` evaluation of `ListDecl.memberOf`, `StringDecl.grammar`, `presence`, `nonempty`, type, and `MapOpenDecl.containerMessage`; following their `vocabulary.ts` doc comments.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts`
+  `{"name":"illegal-unresolved-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"missing\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"missing\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-malformed-id","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-missing-body","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-empty-body","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be nonempty"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be nonempty"}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-nonstring-body","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be a string; got 7"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha.body","message":"context block body must be a string; got 7"}]},"agree":true}`
+  `{"name":"illegal-catalog-not-map","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: Each fixture should produce one finding at its declared grain, with the declaration's exact message (and C7 code for the unresolved ref), and the two channels should agree; all six did.
+
+### SEMANTIC SURPRISE 1 — Wrong-kind catalog suppresses the per-ref resolution finding
+CLASS: SEMANTIC SURPRISE
+WHAT:  A declaration with `ListDecl.memberOf: keysOf("$.contextBlocks")` loads cleanly, but when the catalog is a list the engine emits only the catalog container finding and silently omits the declared per-ref membership finding.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `MembershipRule`/`ListDecl.memberOf` and `engine.ts` selector reliability; following contract C1's explicit statement that catalog failure does not suppress C7.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"wrong-kind-catalog-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks","message":"contextBlocks must be a map; got a list"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: The wrong-kind catalog should yield its one container finding and the issued ref should independently yield `use.refs[0]`, `context block ref "alpha" does not resolve`, code `unresolved_context_block_ref` — two findings — because C1 expressly keeps C7 live; the loaded declaration yielded only one.
+
+### USABILITY 1 — `memberOf keysOf` cannot state entry-belted resolution
+CLASS: USABILITY
+WHAT:  I first expressed C7 with the only apparent ref-to-catalog form, list-level `memberOf` targeting `keysOf("$.contextBlocks")`; it loaded, but a ref to a key whose entry fails `{ body }` was treated as resolved.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `Selector`, `MembershipRule`, and `ListDecl.memberOf`; following contract C7's requirement that key existence alone is not resolution and that the entry must satisfy C3 by reference.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"malformed-entry-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: I expected two findings: the missing-body finding at `contextBlocks.alpha` and the declared unresolved-ref finding at `use.refs[0]`; `defineSurface` emitted no rejection explaining that `keysOf` observes existence only, and the run emitted just the entry finding.
+
+### CONFIRMED 4 — A defaulted optional catalog makes absent-catalog refs decidable
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  Declaring `default: {}` on the optional fixed-map field made an absent catalog behave as an empty catalog for membership without making catalog absence itself illegal.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `NodeBase.default` and `MembershipRule.whenOperandAbsent`; following contract C1/C7 and the documented one-time default materialization.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"absent-catalog-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"alpha\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"alpha\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: The absent catalog should not itself be an error, while each issued ref should still be decidable against an empty key set and fail at its own site; that happened on both channels.
+
+### CONFIRMED 5 — Member-shape failure excludes the member from resolution
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  A grammar-invalid ref member produced only its grammar finding and did not cascade into the list's membership lane.
+WHERE: `engine.ts` list evaluation and `vocabulary.ts` `ListDecl.member` plus `memberOf`; following contract C2/C7's exclusion of shape-failing members from resolution.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"malformed-ref-member","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: Exactly one grammar finding at index 0, with no unresolved-ref cascade, on both channels; that is exactly what happened.
+
+### USABILITY 2 — A new surface needs unrelated substrate scaffolding with no minimal authoring form
+CLASS: USABILITY
+WHAT:  My first scaffolding choice was to import `templateFormat` and reuse its entire `substrate` object because `SurfaceDecl` requires every substrate branch even though this exercise declares only structural value rules.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `SurfaceDecl`/`SubstrateDecl` and `templateFormat.ts`'s surface object; no new-surface construction guidance exists outside the worked example.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts smoke`
+  `{"loaded":true,"name":"legal-resolved","direct":[],"file":[]}`
+EXPECTED: I expected either a documented reusable base/minimal constructor or guidance on whether a new surface should share versus restate substrate policy; there was no diagnostic because copying `templateFormat.substrate` passes the gate, so the worked example—not the construct documentation—supplied that decision.
+
+### CONFIRMED 6 — The load-time gate pinpoints a misspelled selector operand
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  Mutating the working declaration's selector from `$.contextBlocks` to undeclared `$.contextBlockz` was rejected before execution with one precise declaration-site problem.
+WHERE: `defineSurface.ts` selector closure check; following its promise that a declaration carrying an unresolved reference never becomes a surface.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts gate`
+  `SurfaceDeclarationError: the surface declaration is not closed (1 unresolved reference(s)):`
+  `  - $.use.refs.memberOf: selector path "$.contextBlockz" addresses "$.contextBlockz", which the declaration does not declare`
+EXPECTED: One load-time error naming both the declaration site and unresolved target path; the diagnostic did exactly that and directly identified the typo.

+import { parseDocument } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/node_modules/yaml/dist/index.js";
+import { defineSurface } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/defineSurface.ts";
+import { runSurface } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts";
+import { templateFormat } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts";
+import type { NodeDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts";
+
+const rows = ["exercise"] as const;
+
+const blockId: NodeDecl = {
+  kind: "string",
+  tag: "vc-context-block-id",
+  rows,
+  typeMessage: "context block id must be a string; got {value}",
+  nonempty: { message: "context block id must be nonempty" },
+  grammar: {
+    re: "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$",
+    message: "invalid context block id {valueJson}; expected {grammar}",
+  },
+};
+
+const catalog: NodeDecl = {
+  kind: "map.open",
+  tag: "d-context-blocks",
+  rows,
+  default: {},
+  containerMessage: "contextBlocks must be a map; got {value}",
+  keyClass: {
+    kind: "valueClass",
+    tag: "d-context-block-key",
+    rows,
+    valueClass: "blockId",
+    label: "context block id",
+  },
+  keyLaneAt: "container",
+  entry: {
+    kind: "map.fixed",
+    tag: "d-context-block-entry",
+    rows,
+    containerMessage: "context block entry must be a map; got {value}",
+    missingMessage: "context block entry is missing required key {keyJson}",
+    unknownMessage: "unknown context block entry key {keyJson}; allowed: {keys}",
+    fields: {
+      body: {
+        kind: "string",
+        tag: "d-context-block-body",
+        rows,
+        presence: { required: true },
+        typeMessage: "context block body must be a string; got {value}",
+        nonempty: { message: "context block body must be nonempty" },
+      },
+    },
+  },
+};
+
+const refs: NodeDecl = {
+  kind: "list",
+  tag: "d-context-block-refs",
+  rows,
+  containerMessage: "refs must be a list; got {value}",
+  memberLaneAt: "index",
+  member: {
+    kind: "valueClass",
+    tag: "d-context-block-ref-member",
+    rows,
+    valueClass: "blockId",
+    label: "context block ref",
+  },
+  memberOf: {
+    relation: "memberOf",
+    target: { keysOf: "$.contextBlocks" },
+    code: "unresolved_context_block_ref",
+    message: "context block ref {valueJson} does not resolve",
+  },
+};
+
+const declaration: SurfaceDecl = {
+  substrate: templateFormat.substrate,
+  root: {
+    kind: "map.fixed",
+    tag: "d-exercise-root",
+    rows,
+    containerMessage: "document must be a map; got {value}",
+    unknownMessage: "unknown document key {keyJson}; allowed: {keys}",
+    fields: {
+      contextBlocks: catalog,
+      use: {
+        kind: "map.fixed",
+        tag: "d-use",
+        rows,
+        containerMessage: "use must be a map; got {value}",
+        unknownMessage: "unknown use key {keyJson}; allowed: {keys}",
+        fields: { refs },
+      },
+    },
+  },
+  valueClasses: { blockId },
+  crossRules: [],
+  normalizers: [],
+};
+
+const surface = defineSurface(declaration);
+
+type Fixture = { name: string; direct: unknown; yaml: string };
+const fixtures: Fixture[] = [
+  {
+    name: "legal-resolved",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: { body: "hello" } }, use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha:\n    body: hello\nuse:\n  refs:\n    - alpha\n",
+  },
+  { name: "legal-absent-catalog-no-refs", direct: {}, yaml: "{}\n" },
+  { name: "legal-empty-catalog", direct: { contextBlocks: {} }, yaml: "contextBlocks: {}\n" },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-unresolved-ref",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: {}, use: { refs: ["missing"] } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks: {}\nuse:\n  refs: [missing]\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-malformed-id",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { Bad: { body: "x" } } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  Bad:\n    body: x\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-missing-body",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: {} } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha: {}\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-empty-body",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: { body: "" } } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha:\n    body: ''\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-nonstring-body",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: { body: 7 } } },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha:\n    body: 7\n",
+  },
+  {
+    name: "illegal-catalog-not-map",
+    direct: { contextBlocks: [] },
+    yaml: "contextBlocks: []\n",
+  },
+];
+
+function direct(value: unknown) {
+  return runSurface(surface, value, { channel: { kind: "direct" } });
+}
+
+function file(yaml: string) {
+  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+  const value: unknown = doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true });
+  return runSurface(surface, value, { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml } });
+}
+
+if (process.argv[2] === "gate") {
+  const broken = structuredClone(declaration) as any;
+  broken.root.fields.use.fields.refs.memberOf.target = { keysOf: "$.contextBlockz" };
+  try {
+    defineSurface(broken);
+    console.log("unexpectedly loaded");
+  } catch (error) {
+    console.log(String(error));
+  }
+} else if (process.argv[2] === "smoke") {
+  const item = fixtures[0]!;
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ loaded: Object.isFrozen(surface), name: item.name, direct: direct(item.direct).findings, file: file(item.yaml).findings }));
+} else if (process.argv[2] === "probes") {
+  const probes: Fixture[] = [
+    {
+      name: "wrong-kind-catalog-with-ref",
+      direct: { contextBlocks: [], use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+      yaml: "contextBlocks: []\nuse:\n  refs: [alpha]\n",
+    },
+    {
+      name: "malformed-entry-with-ref",
+      direct: { contextBlocks: { alpha: {} }, use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+      yaml: "contextBlocks:\n  alpha: {}\nuse:\n  refs: [alpha]\n",
+    },
+    {
+      name: "absent-catalog-with-ref",
+      direct: { use: { refs: ["alpha"] } },
+      yaml: "use:\n  refs: [alpha]\n",
+    },
+    {
+      name: "malformed-ref-member",
+      direct: { contextBlocks: {}, use: { refs: ["Bad"] } },
+      yaml: "contextBlocks: {}\nuse:\n  refs: [Bad]\n",
+    },
+  ];
+  for (const item of probes) {
+    const d = direct(item.direct);
+    const f = file(item.yaml);
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({
+      name: item.name,
+      direct: { count: d.findings.length, findings: d.findings },
+      file: { count: f.findings.length, findings: f.findings },
+      agree: JSON.stringify(d.findings) === JSON.stringify(f.findings),
+    }));
+  }
+} else {
+  for (const item of fixtures) {
+    const d = direct(item.direct);
+    const f = file(item.yaml);
+    console.log(JSON.stringify({
+      name: item.name,
+      direct: { count: d.findings.length, findings: d.findings },
+      file: { count: f.findings.length, findings: f.findings },
+      agree: JSON.stringify(d.findings) === JSON.stringify(f.findings),
+    }));
+  }
+}

+WHAT:  I first expressed C7 with the only apparent ref-to-catalog form, list-level `memberOf` targeting `keysOf("$.contextBlocks")`; it loaded, but a ref to a key whose entry fails `{ body }` was treated as resolved.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `Selector`, `MembershipRule`, and `ListDecl.memberOf`; following contract C7's requirement that key existence alone is not resolution and that the entry must satisfy C3 by reference.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"malformed-entry-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"contextBlocks.alpha","message":"context block entry is missing required key \"body\""}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: I expected two findings: the missing-body finding at `contextBlocks.alpha` and the declared unresolved-ref finding at `use.refs[0]`; `defineSurface` emitted no rejection explaining that `keysOf` observes existence only, and the run emitted just the entry finding.
+
+### CONFIRMED 4 — A defaulted optional catalog makes absent-catalog refs decidable
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  Declaring `default: {}` on the optional fixed-map field made an absent catalog behave as an empty catalog for membership without making catalog absence itself illegal.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `NodeBase.default` and `MembershipRule.whenOperandAbsent`; following contract C1/C7 and the documented one-time default materialization.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"absent-catalog-with-ref","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"alpha\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"context block ref \"alpha\" does not resolve","code":"unresolved_context_block_ref"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: The absent catalog should not itself be an error, while each issued ref should still be decidable against an empty key set and fail at its own site; that happened on both channels.
+
+### CONFIRMED 5 — Member-shape failure excludes the member from resolution
+CLASS: CONFIRMED
+WHAT:  A grammar-invalid ref member produced only its grammar finding and did not cascade into the list's membership lane.
+WHERE: `engine.ts` list evaluation and `vocabulary.ts` `ListDecl.member` plus `memberOf`; following contract C2/C7's exclusion of shape-failing members from resolution.
+EVIDENCE:
+  `cd /Users/felho/dev/pairflow && pnpm exec tsx /tmp/pairflow-authoring-exercise.ts probes`
+  `{"name":"malformed-ref-member","direct":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"file":{"count":1,"findings":[{"path":"use.refs[0]","message":"invalid context block id \"Bad\"; expected ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$"}]},"agree":true}`
+EXPECTED: Exactly one grammar finding at index 0, with no unresolved-ref cascade, on both channels; that is exactly what happened.
+
+### USABILITY 2 — A new surface needs unrelated substrate scaffolding with no minimal authoring form
+CLASS: USABILITY
+WHAT:  My first scaffolding choice was to import `templateFormat` and reuse its entire `substrate` object because `SurfaceDecl` requires every substrate branch even though this exercise declares only structural value rules.
+WHERE: `vocabulary.ts` `SurfaceDecl`/`SubstrateDecl` and `templateFormat.ts`'s surface object; no new-surface construction guidance exists outside the worked example.

+EXPECTED: I expected either a documented reusable base/minimal constructor or guidance on whether a new surface should share versus restate substrate policy; there was no diagnostic because copying `templateFormat.substrate` passes the gate, so the worked example—not the construct documentation—supplied that decision.

+import type { NodeDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts";
+
+const rows = ["exercise"] as const;
+
+const blockId: NodeDecl = {
+  kind: "string",
+  tag: "vc-context-block-id",
+  rows,
+  typeMessage: "context block id must be a string; got {value}",
+  nonempty: { message: "context block id must be nonempty" },
+  grammar: {
+    re: "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$",
+    message: "invalid context block id {valueJson}; expected {grammar}",
+  },
+};
+
+const catalog: NodeDecl = {
+  kind: "map.open",
+  tag: "d-context-blocks",
+  rows,
+  default: {},
+  containerMessage: "contextBlocks must be a map; got {value}",
+  keyClass: {
+    kind: "valueClass",
+    tag: "d-context-block-key",
+    rows,
+    valueClass: "blockId",
+    label: "context block id",
+  },
+  keyLaneAt: "container",
+  entry: {
+    kind: "map.fixed",
+    tag: "d-context-block-entry",
+    rows,
+    containerMessage: "context block entry must be a map; got {value}",
+    missingMessage: "context block entry is missing required key {keyJson}",
+    unknownMessage: "unknown context block entry key {keyJson}; allowed: {keys}",
+    fields: {
+      body: {
+        kind: "string",
+        tag: "d-context-block-body",
+        rows,
+        presence: { required: true },
+        typeMessage: "context block body must be a string; got {value}",
+        nonempty: { message: "context block body must be nonempty" },
+      },
+    },
+  },
+};
+
+const refs: NodeDecl = {
+  kind: "list",
+  tag: "d-context-block-refs",
+  rows,
+  containerMessage: "refs must be a list; got {value}",
+  memberLaneAt: "index",

+  },
+};
+

+  crossRules: [],
+  normalizers: [],

+
+const surface = defineSurface(declaration);

+
+function direct(value: unknown) {
+  return runSurface(surface, value, { channel: { kind: "direct" } });
+}
+
+function file(yaml: string) {
+  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+  const value: unknown = doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true });
+  return runSurface(surface, value, { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml } });
+}

+    console.log(JSON.stringify({

+      direct: { count: d.findings.length, findings: d.findings },
+      file: { count: f.findings.length, findings: f.findings },
+      agree: JSON.stringify(d.findings) === JSON.stringify(f.findings),

- Elkészült: katalógus + egy feloldó referencialista-pozíció; mindkét csatornán futtatva.

**Képes lenne-e egy kompetens mérnök új surface-t készíteni kizárólag ebből a dokumentációból — és mi lassítaná leginkább?** Egy alapfelületet igen: a deklaráció betöltése, a strukturális szabályok, az alapértelmezések és az egyszerű kulcstagsági feloldás működött. Leginkább az lassítaná, hogy a teljes `substrate`-scaffoldingot a példából kell átvenni, továbbá a selector-szemantika csak alapos forrásolvasással derül ki: a hibás katalóguskonténer elnyomja a referenciatalálatot, a `memberOf keysOf` pedig nem képes kifejezni az entry-validitáshoz kötött feloldást.
